Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tumors module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 691. The projective resolution has length at most 866 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on tumors structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 691. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 866. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 168. QED.
